- [CLS-FOLD-WAF744] STATE:not-started (BOONIES TIER — UNSCHEDULED, LOW PRIORITY; Frank 2026-09-30, eighty-seventh session, with the D138 Q1 ruling: "research [the] waf744 results") — RESEARCH why the caseless-fold VM class form loses on waf744 on x86: match +28% (3.4 vs 2.6 ns/char on a 21-byte subject, the fold won 0 of 11 rounds) and throughput +1.2%, against +6% match on arm64, while the other 9 formchar2 witnesses are tied or fold-faster (docs/dev/lanes/formchar2_report.md §4a/§5; studies/form_char2/results/timing_x86_*.tsv). waf744 is forced-VM only (default-route population 0), so no default artifact is affected. Questions on file: is it the per-site instruction count (atom 1 shorter on x86), a code-layout or alignment effect ([EMIT-ALIGN]), or the short-subject entry regime? FIRST STEP when opened: perf-counter and disassembly comparison of the waf744 fold vs table artifacts on x86. Trigger: a default-route pattern with the waf744 shape, or the [OPTLOOP] cycle reaching class forms. Starts only on Frank's word.

- [EMIT-ALIGN] STATE:not-started (under [OPTLOOP] candidates per D137) (FILED 2026-09-28 by Frank, boonies: "add it as a boonies item. Test it when 'all' our optimizations are in") (**NOT TRIGGERED by O-68's bimodality — bench O-69 @ pcrec-bench e6dfbf6, answering I-116: the per-process split is the CPU FREQUENCY GOVERNOR at launch, not layout. ASLR off does not collapse it; subject-alignment offsets show no dose-response; per-launch clock ratio 1.33 vs 3.32 GHz equals the time ratio; taskset pinning 0/40 slow launches; idle gaps raise the slow fraction. The bench's pinned harness (taskset, ~50 ms loops) is unaffected. Mac scratch timing: warm up before timing, or fresh-launch medians.**) — CODE ALIGNMENT IN THE EMITTED ARTIFACT. ORIGIN: the [B108] placement twin (pcrec-bench O-67, docs/dev/optloop/b108_reading.md addenda) — under `-falign-functions=64 -falign-loops=64` S2a's named-population misses fell inside the null band (aws thr 1.036→1.014, lp-removed thr 1.117→1.029), i.e. the misses were PLACEMENT. That clears S2a; it does NOT show alignment makes anything faster in absolute terms (the twin compared arms, not aligned-vs-unaligned). QUESTION: does emitting alignment for the hot sites win broadly, beyond the null band, at what size cost? SPELLINGS on file: functions — `__attribute__((aligned(N)))` on the emitted entry/body functions (gcc+clang, survives into the user's link; the clean half); loops — no clean per-loop spelling: `-falign-loops` is a caller CFLAGS we do not own (K24's lesson: fixes go in the emitted text), `#pragma GCC optimize`/`__attribute__((optimize(...)))` is documented not-for-production (can reset other flags; clang ignores it), inline-asm `.p2align` at loop heads is fragile beside computed goto. SHAPE if it pays: an emission-wide form row with its own deny flag (D122 addendum 2), an abi event, measured over the WHOLE bench and both ISAs (x86 + aarch64), size priced on the dial (up to N-1 pad bytes per aligned site) — never a per-pattern or per-cell tweak (placement is a lottery that moves with every unrelated change). TRIGGER: LAST, beside [OPT-SIMD] — when the algorithmic optimization queue is in (Frank: "when 'all' our optimizations are in"), so it is measured on the final code, not tuned against layouts that will move. FIRST STEP then: absolute aligned-vs-unaligned per arm across capability (a bench reading; the O-67 builds are the precedent).

- [ENC-MODEL] STATE:not-started (CHARTERED unscheduled by Frank 2026-09-06, fifty-sixth session, boonies — "formalize encoding ascii as an encoding ... byte encodings would be translation tables") — THE FULL ENCODING MODEL: pattern encoding vs subject encoding separated, on the architecture that already exists. (1) THE PATTERN SEMANTIC SPACE IS UNICODE, canonically utf8 source — this is DD-12(1) as ruled (one parser, no encoding in the grammar, CharSet code-point intervals, encoding is a LOWERING instance), restated as the model's spine; [UTF-PAT] (raw utf8 pattern text contract) is its existing near-term half. (2) NON-UTF PATTERN SOURCES are a PRE-COMPILE CONVERSION WRAPPER (source-encoding -> utf8 before the parser), never a parser axis — Frank: "Best handled perhaps pre-compile ... it takes out this whole complication to a wrapper". Named risk to design against: misconverting rx OPERATORS — acute for multi-byte source sets whose trail bytes collide with ASCII metacharacters (Shift-JIS class) and for EBCDIC; single-byte sets are near-trivial. (3) SUBJECT ENCODINGS are TRANSLATION-TABLE BACKENDS on the D58 seam: a single-byte encoding = a 256-entry byte->code-point table (standard mappings exist for ISO-8859-x/Windows-125x/etc.); the lowering intersects the pattern's code-point intervals through the table's image to byte sets; src/gen/enc/'s third-encoding recipe is the landing shape. ASCII FORMALIZED as the first such table encoding (identity on 0x00-0x7F, 0x80+ ill-formed per the encoding's own rules — which also gives \d/\w/POSIX classes their per-encoding statement Frank asked for). (4) THE OPEN RULING, recorded not decided: what \x{hex}/octal DENOTE under a table encoding — a Unicode code point (mapped through the table, refusing points outside its image) or a subject-encoding unit; the split is visible exactly where byte != code point (Windows-1252 0x80 = U+20AC). Frank leans subject-encoding for the byte-valued spellings; the stage-2 per-encoding range-check precedent (byte caps at 0xFF) is the nearest ruled neighbour. D77: no build before a consumer; the row opens on Frank's word, likely after M5.0 completes and [UTF-PAT] lands.
- [FOLD-BASE] STATE:not-started (CHARTERED unscheduled by Frank 2026-09-06, fifty-sixth session, boonies, from reading encoding tables: "a flag analogous to i that folds those characters together for matching") — DIACRITIC/BASE-CHARACTER FOLDING, the caseless architecture's second customer. Two strengths, named apart: CANONICAL EQUIVALENCE (precomposed e-acute == e + U+0301 — one abstract character, two encodings; ICU/Java CANON_EQ is the precedent, Java's notoriously slow at match time) and FOLD-TO-BASE (e-acute == e-grave == e — accent-insensitive, collation primary strength; what Frank asked for; subsumes the first). PCRE2 has NEITHER — a beyond-PCRE2 capability in [M4-SUBST]'s mold, pcrec's own flagged namespace per SR-10. THE MECHANISM IS D23's, WIDENED: caseless folds at class CONSTRUCTION and emits a fold-blind automaton; base-fold is the same move over UCD canonical-decomposition data (vendored at M5.0 stage 3 regardless) — a literal widens to its base-variant set. The wrinkle that keeps it honest: COMBINING SEQUENCES (NFD subjects spell e-acute as TWO code points), so under the flag each literal lowers to [base-variants] followed by mark absorption (\p{M}*-shaped) — still regular, still compiled, which is the AOT wedge: the whole equivalence closure lands in the tables at compile time where runtime engines pay per-match normalization or CANON_EQ blowup. \p relation stated so nobody re-derives it: \p{M} gives the VOCABULARY (talk about marks), never the EQUIVALENCE — this flag is new capability. Caveats for the design round: locale sensitivity at the edges (Swedish aa-ring/o-umlaut are distinct LETTERS; Turkish dotless-i) — first form scopes to locale-agnostic UCD decomposition with the caveat stated, never collation tables; and the flag interacts with `i` (case fold and base fold compose — the combined closure is one constructor pass, not two mechanisms, per the general-mechanisms rule). MECHANISM OPTION, NOT A RULING (Frank proposed it same day and downgraded it same day — "Downgrade my alternations as an option not a ruling"; the design round weighs it against the direct constructor-widening shape): the fold DESUGARS each literal to the ALTERNATION of its variants — the fully general form, expressing multi-code-point variants no class can hold — and [OPT-ALTCLS] (already in the tree, running right after parse, whose stage 1 is exactly single-char-run -> class merging) recovers the efficient class form where recoverable. Zero new engine machinery; the feature is a constructor desugar plus the existing optimizer. The unbounded-mark-stacking nuance rides as one desugared branch of shape base-then-mark-absorption beside the enumerated variants — still alternation, still altcls-visible for its class parts. DEPENDS on stage 3's UCD vendoring + stage 4's fold-closure machinery (this is DD-1's constructor one axis over); D18 earn-its-axis; opens on Frank's word after M5.0 completes.
- [MULTI-PAT] STATE:not-started (CHARTERED unscheduled by Frank 2026-09-06, fifty-sixth session, from the Hyperscan discussion: "note it as an unscheduled row and put it in the boonies") — COMPILE A PATTERN SET INTO ONE SCANNER ARTIFACT, the Hyperscan concept on pcrec's own DFA. A plain alternation of N search patterns already yields the union machine (the same construction Hyperscan starts from) but NOT its results — three semantic gaps, recorded so the row starts honest: (1) PATTERN IDENTITY — an alternation reports "a match", not which branch; the subset construction already knows (a state's accept derives from which branches' accept states are in the set) and pcrec's priority-DFA machinery exists precisely to ERASE that (D3 accept-pruning picks the preferred branch), so the extension is multi-accept TAGGING, a reporting discipline on the existing machine; (2) ALL MATCHES vs leftmost-first non-overlapping — Hyperscan reports every pattern's every match end, overlapping included; PCRE find-all resolves by priority and resumes past the match (foo|foobar on "foobar" reports foo and never foobar) — the all-ends walk is CHEAPER than today's (no reverse pass, no priority resolution; the SOM-is-expensive mirror of [OPT-2]'s reverse-pass finding), so the shape is a new all-ends reporting ENTRY beside search, never a change to search's semantics; (3) STREAMING — save/restore automaton state across buffer boundaries, a property a pure DFA has and today's one-shot artifacts do not expose. Scope: DFA-compilable subset only (faithful to Hyperscan's own restriction); pattern-set INPUT rides the .rxt/definitions machinery that exists; scale caveat stated up front — union determinization is where blowup lives and where Intel's real effort went (literal prefilter decomposition, Teddy/FDR); pcrec's subset-elems/state caps refuse large unions HONESTLY, so the first instantiation is tens of patterns, not Snort-scale. D77: no build before a measured need — a named consumer wanting "scan for any of these N patterns with IDs" is the trigger; the design pass then decides tagging layout, the entry's contract (end offsets only vs paid SOM), and whether streaming is in or out of the first slice. SIBLING ROW: [V-E] (multi-pattern COMPILATION UNITS) is the complementary half — V-E packages N independent matchers into one unit with rx_info as identity; this row fuses N patterns into ONE machine with set semantics. A real consumer likely wants both (V-E's unit surface carrying this row's set scanner as one member).

- [JSONL] STATE:not-started (CHARTERED unscheduled by Frank 2026-09-08 late evening, boonies, from watching pcrecdev2's slow jsonl reader: "jsonl is something that this rx might parse very well... not only can it do it, but it would be about as fast as it could be done") — JSONL AS AN APPLICATION DOMAIN, five tiers stated by Frank: (1) legal-JSONL validator, (2) schema validator for a given schema, (3) general parser via CALLOUTS, (4) known-schema jsonl parser, (5) jsonl-to-csv/etc converter via replace. THE ANALYSIS THAT ORDERS THEM (manager, same night): jsonl's line-per-record property makes the LINE the matching unit, so the usual JSON-isn't-regular objection mostly dissolves; tier 4 LEADS — a fixed schema with bounded nesting is a nearly-regular line language compilable onto the DFA/hybrid rungs with capture slots as the field extractor, which is where the as-fast-as-possible claim is strongest AND where the NAMED CONSUMER lives (the bench's store/*.jsonl record-schema reader, measured slow by its own operator — the D77 trigger is already half-met; ask the bench for the reader's baseline numbers + the schema when the row opens). Tier 1 general legality needs module `recursion`'s (?1) machinery (exists; VM rungs — honest boundary: a hand parser competes there; DEPTH-BOUNDED validation unrolls back toward the fast rungs and real schemas are depth-bounded). Tier 5 rides [M4-SUBST]'s direction. Tier 3 needs a NEW drop-in module `callouts` (PCRE2-compatible surface; the AOT form compiles a callout to a DIRECT CALL in the emitted C — no interpreter indirection, a beyond-PCRE2-quality story and a D18 earn-its-axis candidate). Sibling rows: [MULTI-PAT] (a schema set as one scanner), [V-E] (shipping a set of schema matchers behind the organizer). Opens on Frank's word after a design pass; first instantiation = the bench reader's schema, benchmarked against their current python reader.
- [WORD-FOLD] STATE:not-started (under [OPTLOOP] candidates per D137) (CHARTERED unscheduled by Frank 2026-09-11, sixtieth session, boonies, INVESTIGATION, from the cls-fold/cube discussion: "8 byte masks then compare?") — WORD-WIDE CUBE COMPARE FOR LITERAL RUNS: the study's per-position cube analysis (cube_of over the bare set) vectorized ACROSS positions — for an 8-byte literal window where every position's accepted set is a one-cube, THE CANONICAL FORM IS THE AND-MASK (Frank, 2026-09-11, superseding the first OR spelling): `memcpy(&w, subject+pos, 8); (w & K) == T` with per-byte K = ~freebits and T = base & K — fold DOWN, not up — which accepts exactly the cross-product of the per-position cubes AND makes every byte kind one vocabulary: exact byte K=0xFF, caseless letter K=0xDF, any one-cube class K=~free, and a byte PAST THE LITERAL'S END is the degenerate all-bits-free cube K=0x00/T=0x00 — so a short window's tail IS the same test as the rest (load+and+cmp, 3 ops uniformly), not a special case sharing code (sound and exact; case-exact positions mask 0x00, caseless ASCII 0x20, every other one-cube class participates identically — general mechanism, not a caseless special case). MEASURED BASELINE (2026-09-11, emitted artifacts read directly): (a) the VM emits a literal chain as ONE INSTRUCTION PER BYTE — bounds check + single-byte compare + goto per position, both casings (caseless = the same chain with per-byte |0x20) — so an 8-wide window collapses 8 bounds checks + 8 compares + 8 dispatches to 1+1+1; FUSIBILITY PRECONDITION: no label inside the window may be a live resume target (statically knowable; [OPT-VMFL]'s census puts 35% of VM artifacts entirely frameless where it holds trivially). (b) The DFA's offset-k candidate-start skip (rx_ofsskip, docs/design/offset_k_skip.md) DEGRADES TO NOTHING under (?i) — memchr needs one exact byte, a caseless offset has two, so the caseless twin loses the whole mechanism; the cheapest scan-side win is a SELECTION PREFERENCE (scan a case-invariant offset — digits/punct/_ never fold — and cube-compare only the candidate checks), needing no new scan machinery; two-memchr and SWAR-scan are the fallbacks where no invariant offset exists. SCOPE: byte-domain cubes only (ASCII case IS the 0x20 bit; utf8 multi-byte folds were never byte cubes — same boundary D23's construction-time fold draws); NO pattern-length tail ladder (Frank, 2026-09-11 same day; subsumed by the AND-mask canonical form above): every literal length 1..8 is the one `(w & K) == T` compare and longer literals are ceil(len/8) of the identical shape, uniformly; the SUBJECT-end bound is the separate, surviving guard (the 8-byte load reads subject[pos..pos+7] and pcrec does not own the buffer — the page-boundary trick real engines use fails the both-axes ASan/UBSan discipline), reduced to: wide compares wherever pos+8<=n, per-byte fallback only inside the subject's final <8 bytes (fixed-cost epilogue, independent of pattern length; K27-style, UBSan-verified both directions); memcpy loads + M/T/K built from static byte arrays (alignment-UB-free, endian-neutral); non-cube positions split the window or fall back. D77 GATE — THE CENSUS FIRST: count corpus+bench literal runs >=4-8 bytes with >=1 cube-but-not-singleton position (Frank's own caution recorded: the shipped corpus may contain NO example — "there may be scenarios" — so the census enumerates scenario classes too, e.g. caseless keywords/header names, and overlaps [FORM-CHAR2] (ii)'s hot-fold-constant question); a zero-population census parks the row without prejudice. SIBLING: [CLS-TREE] (this is a SEQUENCE-level kit member built on the same cube_of; the design note may cite it but is NOT gated on it). SWAR in portable uint64 C — the scan-edge/mask-test register-width family, not SIMD; does not jump the SIMD-last queue. **S4 C0+C1 BUILT 2026-10-03 (lane s4build): P2 (the cube) and the run compare's exact rows landed; the MASK form is not built yet — it enters with C3 (caseless necessary run, panel pending); the VM masked run (C2) is HELD (no losing VM-route caseless cell). The gcc-15/x86 arm is step 0 of docs/dev/optloop/s4/alpha_c1.sh, owed via the executor.**

NS/CHAR ROUTING RULED (Frank, 2026-09-11, sixtieth session): the darwin load1<0.5 gate stays UNTOUCHED (calibrated on headless ubuntubudu; unreachable on a desktop Mac — 19 samples min 0.71; darwin timing is never citable, scratch-directional at most); the ns/char run executes on ubuntubudu, riding the I-65 executor session (`make -C studies/cls_tree_study bench CC=gcc`, bench.py's own gate passes there). Both 2026-09-11 charter amendments were in the brief verbatim; Constraint 1 discharged as measurement, Constraint 2 closed by its own gate.) (CHARTERED by Frank 2026-09-10 ~03:0x, from \P{Unknown}'s week: "We need a class implementation that is a binary tree of ranges") — THE THIRD CLASS-EMISSION FORM: a huge class emitted as DATA (the A_CLASS sorted interval set as a compact rodata table, 8 bytes/interval) with membership by BINARY SEARCH over ranges (~log2(n) compares per character), instead of automaton STRUCTURE whose size grows with interval count. The D77 trigger is already MEASURED: the K53 family (six spellings refuse at default axes incl. \p{L} utf8, 1,076,640 bytes) and K55 (\P{Unknown}'s 729 intervals = 689,367 VM bytes vs the 500,000 cap) exist ONLY because today's forms encode intervals as byte-machine structure — the tree form prices \P{Unknown} at ~5.8KB rodata + ~10 compares. DESIGN PASS DECIDES: (1) the VM instruction shape (decode-next-char, search, advance — the natural host; stage 4's caseless-backref residual is the decode-in-artifact precedent and its ill-formed rule is the correctness seam, inherited verbatim: the class test's decoder rejects exactly the automaton's ill-formed set); (2) the DFA/hybrid seam (a byte-wise DFA cannot search mid-state — tree-classes as a rung/edge in the hybrid, not forced into states); (3) DIAL PLACEMENT ([OPT-DIAL]'s threshold shape: bitmap/compares small, byte-automaton medium — fastest per char — tree huge; the crossover interval-count is a MEASUREMENT); (4) caseless composition (the fold applies at construction per stage 4's per-contribution rule — the tree stores the folded interval set, no runtime fold); (5) what it RETIRES (most of tests/known_fail/k53_uprops_oversize.rxt + K55's witness stop refusing; [K53-SELRETRY] stays as selection-layer correctness — sibling, not superseded). ADDENDUM (Frank, minutes later: "Or something other than a bit array. Because that size is crazy"): THE REPRESENTATION IS OPEN — tree-of-ranges is the SEED, not the decision. (Precision for the record: the crazy sizes are not a bit array today — byte classes use 32-byte bitmaps; the megabytes are AUTOMATON STRUCTURE, intervals decomposed into byte-sequence states/instructions — which is why any class-as-DATA layout collapses them.) Candidate layouts the design pass measures against the real 312 script sets + the K53 six, on size × per-char cost: (a) sorted interval table + binary search (the seed; Eytzinger/cache-optimal ordering as a variant); (b) STAGED PAGE TABLES (PCRE2's own property-lookup shape: plane→page index→per-page 32-byte bitmap leaves — O(1) per char, dense-page-friendly); (c) interval/bitmap HYBRID (intervals for sparse spans, bitmap leaves for dense pages — likely the size winner on real script data, which is bursty); (d) branchless/SIMD inversion-list probe if timing demands it. The chosen form is a MEASURED pick, one mechanism (no per-class special cases; the dial threshold selects form by class shape). Engine tier (opus), design note + light panel before implementation; opens after [K53-SELRETRY] lands or on Frank's word. **REFRAMED 2026-09-10 (Frank) from "a third form for huge classes" to THE GENERAL SHAPE: a class matcher is COMPOSED PER-SECTION from a small kit of representations, STATICALLY SELECTED.** A class is a statically-known interval set; partition it into runs at compile time and emit the CHEAPEST test per run — a dense ≤64-code-point span → a single uint64 mask test (NO memory load; [FORM-CHAR] STEP 0 measured branchless mask+compare beating a table load when the run fits a register), a sparse scatter → 2-3 range compares, a huge contiguous block → a bound check, a wide table-worthy region → the tree/page-table/bitmap-leaf (the original [CLS-TREE] payload, now just ONE kit member). Applies to BYTE classes too, not only huge/\p ones: a degenerate `[...]` that today gets a flat 32-byte bitmap may be two 64-bit-fittable runs → two mask tests, no load. The dial ([OPT-DIAL]) keys the PER-SECTION choice, not one form per whole class; because we see the class statically and emit C, the matcher is bespoke straight-line code (the cheapest test per section), not a general-purpose membership loop. SIBLINGS FOLDED IN: [FORM-CHAR]/[FORM-CHAR2] (the caseless class-vs-fold measurements) and [OPT-CLSPACK] are the same kit's members measured piecemeal — the design pass unifies rather than adding a parallel mechanism (general-mechanisms rule). THE STUDY (Frank: "can we test algorithms?" — YES, the prototype-before-commit shape): a read-only study lane builds each candidate representation as a standalone matcher and benchmarks membership (size × ns/char, both engines, cache footprint) over the REAL populations — the 312 script sets + the K53 six + a representative byte-class sample — and the SECTIONING algorithm itself (how to partition an interval set into runs, and the per-run selection rule) is a measured output, not an assumption. Study first (no src/), then the design note picks the kit + the sectioning rule from its numbers, then implementation. **+2 PLACEMENT DIRECTION (Frank, 2026-09-16 ~23:4x EDT, at the dial table's ratification): the kit's EXTREME-SPEED forms — full-table/huge-bitmap class membership, the ~227-294KB-object-bytes family the study measured, its "few huge bitmaps at 5.6x size for 6 probe ops" notch — are +2's INTENDED CONTENT, not a place the dial stops before. The study's own §4 caution ("the dial should stop before the extreme notch") is superseded IN DIRECTION by the D103-addendum art-over-science ruling: +2 exists exactly for ridiculous-but-measured trades chosen deliberately. The design note prices that notch as a real +2 lambda position (with its measured rate cited), rather than excluding it.** **SCOPE ADDITION 2026-09-25 (Frank, eightieth session): the class COMPILE-TIME work joins this effort — "hit everything class related at the same time like the class tree work"; after the open queue.** K67 (`\p{L}+` -e utf8: 77 s pcrec compile) carries the measured need: (1) the size-cap retry ladder's 3× rebuild is GENERAL and filed separately as [OPT-RETRY-REUSE] (→ ~26 s here); (2) the 26 s one-pass cost is mostly the GENERAL loop-context closure path, filed as [OPT-CLOSURE-CTX]; what stays HERE is the class's own share — the utf8 class lowers to a flat 600+-branch alternation (no prefix sharing), which multiplies every per-branch closure cost. Frank 2026-09-25: "we could set it up as a single indexed bit array. One for --tune" — i.e. the class as ONE node backed by an indexed bit array at the speed end of the --tune dial (cls_tree_study.md's policy table already has BITMAP/PAGE64 sections chosen per speed-vs-size policy; the bit array applies where code points are decoded — the VM, the emitted class test — while the byte DFA gains from the class being one node instead of the fan-out). Design together with the tree/kit matcher work. NOTE (Frank 2026-09-25): the study's kit is ENGINE-EXTERNAL — a code-point predicate whose sections/alternations are not VM nodes or DFA states; the SHIPPED utf8 lowering is not (it expands the class into the NFA as a flat alternation). So two pieces: the kit as the predicate (VM one-instruction decode+test, emitted class tests, --tune's λ), and for the byte DFA a compact, prefix/suffix-shared byte automaton built outside the engine and spliced in (bare \p{L}'s whole DFA is 299 states vs the 2,711-node flat NFA alternation). **S0 COMPLETE (D131, 2026-09-29; lane clsfit, calibration results `docs/design/cls_tree_design.md` §1.7): the λ table is RULED — one kit constant K (λ=4) plus a first-match rule over the whole-set tables (page3w/page2w/bitmap1), superseding the five pinned frontier constants (`docs/design/opt_dial_design.md` §4, `docs/spec/tuning.md`'s λ row, both applied at D131). The refitted per-probe model (branch mispredicts, ~3.9 ns each) replaces the old λ·Σops term the ubuntubudu run refuted a second time (member r +0.08 → +0.98, 30/36 kit orderings). S1 CHARTERED (lane clss1), runs alongside [UCP] U2 (disjoint: the kit lives in src/, no emitter calls it yet). S2 RE-SCOPED (D131 item 5): D129 Q5's premise is amended — under a fair `--dispatch switch` re-run the kit's byte tier is the SLOWEST byte form at every N (+13/+16/+19% vs the bitmap, O-77), so the kit's byte forms are a SIZE-leaning `--tune` position only; the default byte-class form stays a table (see [OPT-CLSPACK] below, PROMOTED separately).** **TRIGGER for [ART-POSS-ARMS] (2026-10-07, rev 2.1 R-7): when the kit-sized UCP `\w` route lands under `-e utf8 --ucp` (the refusal naming "[CLS-TREE] S4 / [UCP] U3" goes away), re-sweep the arms' `utf,ucp` rows — `docs/design/poss_arms_measurements/rev21/r21_claimmark.py` pins their REFUSED count (`UCP_PIN`) and exits 3 when it moves (poss_arms.md §8.3).**

- [CTX-PREFILTER] STATE:not-started (under [OPTLOOP] candidates per D137) **STEP 0 DELIVERED 2026-09-29 (lane lacens2, docs/dev/ctx_prefilter_census.md): 143 still-VM patterns carry a positive multi-char lookaround (only 6 bench-derived); 111/115 with a sound necessary byte are NARROW; the tightening is an INDEPENDENCE MODEL, not measured. Verdict: do not build from this alone; a joint-position measurement is the D77 trigger.** **JOINT-POSITION MEASUREMENT DELIVERED 2026-09-29 (lane ctxjoint, docs/dev/ctx_prefilter_joint.md, studies/ctx_prefilter_joint/): over 99 applicable patterns x 6 subjects (bench capability + syntax throughput texts, sha-verified) the condition rejects 60-77% of live candidates (false candidates removed 96.5-100%; T ⊆ C1 ⊆ C0 in 594/594 cells; controls pass) and the independence model's per-pattern gap is 0.21-0.37 in both directions; NO D77 trigger met — the one real bench construct clearing the removal bar (lka-pos/lka-verb, 59% at 1.58/KB) has a per-byte loss (auto == forced-VM, 1.21 ns/B), so [OPT-HYB-RESEED] is upstream; re-open after it lands and a lookaround cell still loses.** (FILED 2026-09-29 by Frank, eighty-fifth session, UNSCHEDULED; follows [UCP] U2's merge) — A LOOKAROUND LOWERS TO THE STRONGEST ONE-CHARACTER CONTEXT THE MACHINE CAN HOST. Today (src/ir/nfa.c:690) every A_LOOK becomes N_EPS in the NFA. A lookaround pattern is VM-forced, so the only DFA built from it is the hybrid's PREFILTER, which is the lookaround-free superset: sound rejection and a sound span start, but the lookaround contributes no rejection power (and Vm.mrl_win stays excluded). U2's A_CTX node carries a one-character context set. The general rule: EXACT when the body is one character (U2, the real DFA); for a longer POSITIVE body, a NECESSARY one-character condition, in the PREFILTER ONLY (`(?=abc)` → next ∈ {a}; `(?<=xy)` → prev ∈ {y}; `(?=\d+px)` → next ∈ \d), which is still a superset, only tighter. Negative multi-character bodies imply nothing and stay N_EPS. It does NOT lift the mrl_win exclusion (span end is still not an upper bound). As a T3 row pair (the prefilter-only approximate row after the exact row), it is a first-match table with no special case. STEP 0 (D77, Mac, cheap): a census over docs/dev/lookaround_census (493) + the bench's VM-only lookaround patterns of positive lookarounds whose first/last-character set is NARROW, plus how many false candidates their prefilters admit today. Build only if the census shows a population whose prefilter would materially tighten. [2026-10-03 bench O-81 item 2: the lka-pos auto-vs-forced-VM divergence is CONFIRMED on the bench (auto slower at the two smaller throughput subjects, faster at the largest; lka-verb never inverts); the density framing stays untestable until a density-controlled subject pair exists (syntax's throughput subjects carry 3/6/27 matches on 64K/256K/1M) — see [BENCH-ASKS-PENDING].] **ROUND 3 rank 3b-7 (D153): WAITS for refactor B [DEC-FALLBACK] (prefilter admission; Q16 ruled).**
- [LIST-CLASSES] STATE:not-started (FILED 2026-09-28 by Frank, eighty-fifth session, UNSCHEDULED: "File an item to --list the class names/compositions. Remember that there may be more than the two encodes. Therefore encode is a column. Can be long and exhaustive- it's a debugging tool") — A `--list-classes` DEBUGGING LISTING of every named class and its composition. One row per (class spelling × ENCODING × UCP/restriction configuration). ENCODING IS A COLUMN, never a per-encoding section or flag: encodings beyond byte/utf8 are expected ([UTF-RW], latin1, …), so the listing iterates the encoding registry and never names the encodings it knows. Rows cover every escape (\d \s \w \h \v \R …, negations), every POSIX class, every \p{…} property/script/general category, the UCP (D130) and (?a…)-restricted variants, and the caseless-folded forms. Columns: spelling, encoding, config (UCP, restriction knob, caseless), the resolved composition (the interval set in code points, plus the definitions-table/DEF_UCP row that produced it and its spelled composition, e.g. how [:graph:] is built), interval count, member count, and optionally the form the [CLS-TREE] kit/dial picks per --tune position. ALIASES (Frank 2026-09-28, "there are names like Cyrillic"): every accepted spelling of a \p set is its own row — script names (\p{Cyrillic}), Script=/sc= long and short forms (\p{sc=Cyrl}), script extensions (\p{scx:Cyrillic}), category long/short names — with SPELLING and CANONICAL SET as separate columns, so aliases that should agree visibly resolve to one set. Recognised-but-unimplemented properties (probe 2026-09-28: \p{Bidi_Class=L} → "not implemented yet") are rows too, marked unimplemented — the listing shows the gaps as well as the coverage. Exhaustive and long by design (thousands of rows — a debugging tool, not a user summary), filterable by a spelling/encoding argument. Complements --list-definitions (the rows) and --list-syntax (the spellings); part of the [LIST-TABLES] family. Spec hunk (D80) in docs/spec/cli.md when built. Natural home: after [UCP] U1 lands (the configurations exist then).

A collapse rung ([SEL-1] `sel1-collapse`, or the size-cap `prefilter-collapse`) is offered today on attempts it cannot help. critB1's THREE-WAY SPLIT of the attempts it buys: (i) no collapsible repeat (`!pfc_rep`, the survey §4.1 drift: the collapsed lowering IS the exact one, so the rebuilt machine fails again); (ii) nullable but NOT `empty_admits` (T2 row 5 reads `empty_admits` and keeps the prefilter, T3 row 3 reads bare `nullable` and declines the collapse, so the exact machine that just failed is rebuilt and fails again; live at shipped limits: `^(?:(?:a|b)*a(?:a|b){20})?$` 3 attempts `overflowed-dfa`, `-e utf8 ^(\p{Xwd}{1,3})?$` 3 attempts `size-cap-retry`); (iii) `empty_admits`, [OPT-4.1]'s DESIGNED `declined-nullable` decline, not waste. PRECEDENT: `run_axes.sh`'s own `AXES=` env var is the existing MANUAL form of this idea (a human names the subset to re-run); this row is that mechanism made automatic and keyed rather than hand-invoked. **MUST NOT WEAKEN THE GATE**: a marker is invalid — and its unit re-runs — on a dirty tree, or on ANY flag/toolchain change (compiler version, `CC`, `GENCFLAGS`, `HARNESS_BATCH`, the sanitizer flag set) even if the tree SHA is unchanged, per the check-design lesson that a control sharing a source with what it controls is no control at all (docs/dev/learnings.md §3).
